### [1] [EchoSR: Efficient Context Harnessing for Lightweight Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17470)
*Hanli Zhao,Binhao Wang,Shihao Zhao,Tao Wang,Kaihao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EchoSR是一个面向轻量级图像超分辨率的高效上下文捕获框架，通过解耦局部、多尺度和全局建模并结合跨尺度重叠融合机制，在多个基准上以约2倍速度超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轻量级超分辨率方法在平衡重建保真度与计算效率方面存在挑战，许多方法虽尝试扩大感受野但计算开销大、核大小缩放简单或缺乏多尺度集成机制，限制了有效性与可扩展性。

Method: EchoSR将特征学习解耦为局部、多尺度和全局三个独立建模阶段，通过高效上下文捕获策略实现，并引入跨尺度重叠融合机制促进无缝的跨尺度集成。

Result: 在多个基准上，EchoSR在性能上持续优于最先进的轻量级超分辨率方法，同时推理速度提升约2倍。

Conclusion: EchoSR通过解耦多尺度建模与层级上下文融合，有效解决了轻量级超分辨率中感受野扩展与计算效率之间的矛盾，实现了性能与速度的双重提升。

Abstract: Image super-resolution (SR) aims to reconstruct high-quality, high-resolution (HR) images from low-resolution (LR) inputs and plays a critical role in various downstream applications. Despite recent advancements, balancing reconstruction fidelity and computational efficiency remains a fundamental challenge, particularly in resource-constrained scenarios. While existing lightweight methods attempt to expand receptive fields, many of them either incur substantial computational overhead, naively scale up kernel sizes, or lack mechanisms for coherent multi-scale integration, limiting their overall effectiveness and scalability. To address these limitations, we propose EchoSR, an efficient context-harnessing framework for lightweight image super-resolution, which unifies multi-scale receptive field modeling and hierarchical context fusion. EchoSR decouples feature learning into disentangled local, multi-scale, and global modeling stages through an efficient context-harnessing strategy, and further promotes seamless cross-scale integration via a cross-scale overlapping fusion mechanism. Extensive experiments have shown that EchoSR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art lightweight super-resolution methods across multiple benchmarks, while also achieving a faster speed $(\sim 2\times)$. The source code is available at \url{https://github.com/funnyWang-Echoes/EchoSR}.

</details>


### [2] [Omni-Customizer: End-to-End MultiModal Customization for Joint Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17488)
*Yuheng Chen,Qingdong He,Teng Hu,Yuji Wang,Yabiao Wang,Lizhuang Ma,Jiangning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Omni-Customizer，一个端到端的联合音视频定制生成框架，通过Omni-Context Fusion模块、Masked TTS Cross-Attention机制和Semantic-Anchored Multimodal RoPE等技术，实现多主体视觉身份和语音音色的同步保存与融合，在双模态定制生成中达到SOTA表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型在联合音视频生成方面取得进展，但同时在多个交互主体间保持视觉身份和语音音色的多模态定制尚未充分探索，缺乏端到端的解决方案。

Method: 提出Omni-Customizer框架，包含：(1) Omni-Context Fusion (OCF)模块，用密集多模态身份线索丰富文本提示；(2) Masked TTS Cross-Attention (MTP-CA)机制，防止"语音泄露"问题；(3) Semantic-Anchored Multimodal RoPE (SA-MRoPE)，将音视频参考令牌对齐到语义描述；(4) 交错音视频调度训练策略适配多语言场景；(5) 渐进式成对到跨对课程学习。

Result: 在视觉身份相似度、音色一致性、音视频同步精度和整体音视频保真度方面达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: Omni-Customizer有效实现了多模态身份信息的精确绑定与无缝融合，为联合音视频定制生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: The landscape of joint audio and video generation has been fundamentally transformed by the advent of powerful foundation models. Despite these strides, achieving cohesive multimodal customization for the simultaneous preservation of visual identities and vocal timbres across multiple interacting subjects remains largely underexplored. To bridge this gap, we present Omni-Customizer, an end-to-end framework targeted at the precise binding and seamless fusion of multimodal identity information. Specifically, we introduce an Omni-Context Fusion (OCF) module that effectively enriches the base textual prompt with dense, multimodal identity cues, along with a Masked TTS Cross-Attention (MTP-CA) mechanism explicitly designed to prevent the severe "speech leakage" problem. Within this architecture, we propose Semantic-Anchored Multimodal RoPE (SA-MRoPE) to anchor visual and audio reference tokens, along with TTS embeddings, to their corresponding semantic descriptions, enabling structured multimodal fusion and robust identity binding. Furthermore, we devise a comprehensive training strategy that incorporates interleaved audio-video scheduling to rapidly adapt the audio branch to multilingual scenarios without degrading foundational priors, and a progressive in-pair to cross-pair curriculum to facilitate the learning of high-level and robust identity features. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Omni-Customizer achieves state-of-the-art performance in dual-modal customized generation, excelling across visual identity similarity, timbre consistency, precise audio-video synchronization, and overall video-audio fidelity.

</details>


### [3] [Noise2Params: Unification and Parameter Determination from Noise via a Probabilistic Event Camera Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16317)
*Owen Root,Julinda Mujo,Min Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于光子统计学的统一概率模型，用于描述事件相机（EC）的事件检测过程，将静态场景噪声事件和阶跃响应曲线（S曲线）统一在同一分析框架下。基于该模型，提出Noise2Params方法，通过最小化与观测噪声事件分布的误差，确定相机特定参数（对数对比度阈值B、流明-光子转换因子α和泄漏项θ），仅需静态均匀场景记录。实验表明，利用合成噪声图像训练的CNN优于仅用实验数据训练的CNN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机的精确统一模型长期缺失，阻碍了标定和算法设计。现有方法无法将静态场景噪声事件和S曲线统一描述，且参数估计通常需要专用动态光源，难以实验获取。

Method: 基于光子统计学的概率模型，推导了精确泊松、鞍点和高斯三种概率分布形式；提出Noise2Params方法，通过最小化模型预测与观测噪声事件分布的误差，估计参数B、α和θ（发现θ依赖于强度）；利用合成噪声图像训练CNN进行验证。

Result: Noise2Params仅需静态均匀场景即可估计相机参数；发现泄漏项θ具有强度依赖性；CNN在合成噪声图像上训练后，对静态场景的重建性能优于仅使用实验数据训练的CNN。

Conclusion: 该框架为事件相机标定、噪声感知算法设计以及光子受限场景下的应用提供了定量基础，统一了静态噪声和动态响应的建模。

Abstract: Accurate, unified models for event cameras (ECs) remain elusive, hampering calibration and algorithm design. We develop a foundational probabilistic model for EC event detection, grounded in photon statistics, that unifies the description of static scene noise events and step response curves (S-curves) within a single analytical framework. Three formulations of the probability distributions are derived, spanning all intensity regimes: exact Poisson, saddle-point, and Gaussian. The model reveals the underlying connection between these otherwise disparate EC behaviors and clarifies the interpretation of S-curves, which we show is more nuanced than selecting a fixed probability threshold. Based on this model, we propose Noise2Params, a method for determining camera-specific values of the log-contrast threshold $B$, the lux-to-photon conversion factor $α$, and the leakage term $θ$ (found to be intensity dependent), via error minimization against observed noise-event distributions. Noise2Params requires only recordings of static, uniform scenes, offering an experimentally accessible alternative to approaches that demand specialized dynamic light sources. We further support the validity the model by training convolutional neural networks (CNNs) on synthetic noise images generated from our distributions and evaluating their ability to reconstruct static scenes from experimental data. We further demonstrate the utility of our model by showing that CNNs incorporating synthetic data outperform those trained solely on experimental data. Our framework provides a quantitative foundation for EC calibration, noise-aware algorithm design, and applications in photon-limited regimes.

</details>


### [4] [StrLoRA: Towards Streaming Continual Visual Instruction Tuning for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16353)
*Chang Che,Ziqi Wang,Hui Ma,Cheems Wang,Zenglin Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出StrCVIT设置和StrLoRA方法，解决多模态大模型在连续数据流中的持续视觉指令微调问题，通过任务感知专家选择和跨模态注意力加权，有效提升模型在动态混合任务流中的学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CVIT方法局限于任务增量设置（每阶段单一任务），无法应对现实中数据以连续流形式到达、任务动态混合的真实场景，需弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出StrLoRA，一个正则化的两阶段专家路由框架：1）任务感知专家选择（基于文本指令激活稀疏专家子集）；2）令牌级专家加权（通过局部视觉令牌与全局指令表示的跨模态注意力计算贡献权重）；3）路由稳定性正则化（对齐当前路由分布与历史指数移动平均参考）。

Result: 在构建的StrCVIT基准上，StrLoRA大幅优于现有方法。

Conclusion: StrLoRA有效增强了模型从持续演化的数据流中学习的能力。

Abstract: Continual Visual Instruction Tuning (CVIT) enables Multimodal Large Language Models to incrementally acquire new abilities. However, existing CVIT methods operate under a restrictive task-incremental setting, where each training phase corresponds to a single, predefined task. This does not reflect real-world conditions, where data arrives as a continuous stream of interleaved and dynamically evolving tasks. To bridge this gap, we introduce Streaming CVIT (StrCVIT), a more general and realistic setting where models learn from a stream of data chunks containing a dynamic mixture of tasks. In StrCVIT, a model must simultaneously acquire new abilities, reinforce recurring abilities, and mitigate forgetting. Existing CVIT methods fail here as they cannot reliably distinguish or adapt to the heterogeneous task samples within each chunk. We therefore propose StrLoRA, a regularized two-stage expert routing framework. StrLoRA first performs task-aware expert selection using the textual instruction to activate a sparse subset of relevant experts, reducing cross-task interference. It then applies token-wise expert weighting within this subset, where contribution weights are computed via cross-modal attention between local visual tokens and the global instruction representation. To maintain stability across the non-stationary stream, a routing-stability regularization aligns current routing distributions with a historical exponential moving average reference. Extensive experiments on a newly developed StrCVIT benchmark show that StrLoRA substantially outperforms existing methods, effectively enhancing model's abilities from continuously evolving data streams.

</details>


### [5] [How Many Visual Tokens Do Multimodal Language Models Need? Scaling Visual Token Pruning with F^3A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16359)
*YiJie Huang,Yiqun Zhang,Zhuoyue Jia,Xiaocui Yang,Junzhao Huang,Zihan Wang,Shi Feng,Daling Wang,Yifei Zhang,Yongkang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出F^3A方法，一种无需训练的视觉令牌剪枝路由器，通过任务条件化的证据搜索，在固定视觉令牌预算下高效分配视觉令牌，以降低多模态大模型的推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型随视觉序列增长导致推理成本上升，现有训练无关剪枝方法（如注意力、相似性等）未能将剪枝视为任务条件化的证据搜索，尤其在激进压缩和跨模型尺度下效果不佳。

Method: F^3A：在语言模型消费图像令牌前构建轻量级问题条件化线索，通过冻结稀疏感知头与视觉网格令牌匹配，经粗粒度证据定位、局部细化、覆盖保持竞争、未覆盖区域恢复四个阶段分配固定视觉令牌预算，无需训练和额外LLM前向传播。

Result: 实验验证了该训练无关方法在激进压缩下的有效性，但具体数值结果未在摘要中明确给出。

Conclusion: 视觉令牌剪枝应被视为任务条件化的证据搜索，F^3A通过轻量级路由机制在无需额外训练和推理开销下实现高效剪枝，保持了原有多模态提示和解码流程。

Abstract: Vision-language models improve perception by feeding increasingly long visual token sequences into language backbones, but the resulting inference cost raises a basic scaling question: as multimodal models grow, how many visual tokens are actually needed, and how should they be allocated under a fixed visual token budget? Existing training-free pruning methods typically answer this with one-shot proxies such as decoder attention, visual similarity, or conditional diversity. We argue that visual token pruning is better viewed as task-conditioned evidence search, especially under aggressive compression and across model scales. We propose F^3A, a training-free router for visual token pruning that operates before the language model consumes image tokens. F^3A builds lightweight question-conditioned cues, matches them to visual-grid tokens through frozen sparse sensing heads, and allocates a fixed vision token budget via coarse evidence localization, local refinement, coverage-preserving competition, and recovery of under-covered regions. It requires no model training, no extra LLM forward pass and preserves the original multimodal prompting and decoding pipeline.

</details>


### [6] [Fre-Res: Frequency-Residual Video Token Compression for Efficient Video MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16366)
*Yigui Feng,Qinglin Wang,Yang Liu,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Fre-Res提出了一种预算自适应的双轨视频令牌压缩框架，通过分离空间锚点（高保真）和时间残差频率令牌（紧凑编码动态变化），在保持视频理解精度的同时大幅减少视觉令牌数量，实现了精度与效率的优化平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频多模态大模型面临空间保真度与时间覆盖范围之间的固有矛盾：保留细粒度视觉细节需要大量空间令牌，而捕捉短暂动态事件则需要密集的时间采样，现有方法难以兼顾两者。

Method: Fre-Res采用双轨压缩策略：(1) 保留稀疏高保真空间锚点；(2) 对帧间残差轨迹在视觉隐空间应用时间一维DCT，利用低频集中特性生成紧凑的残差频率令牌；并通过空间引导吸收器（Spatial-Guided Absorber）将时间残差信息注入对应的空间锚点令牌中。

Result: 在细粒度短视频和长视频推理基准上，Fre-Red在显著减少视觉令牌长度的同时，匹配或接近全令牌性能，实现了有利的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 实验表明，时间频率残差能够保留因果转换线索，而空间锚点对于细粒度物体和布局推理仍然不可或缺，验证了双轨分离设计的有效性。

Abstract: Video MLLMs face a persistent tension between spatial fidelity and temporal coverage: preserving fine-grained visual details requires many spatial tokens, while capturing short-lived events requires dense temporal sampling. We propose \textbf{Fre-Res}, a budget-adaptive dual-track video-token compression framework that separates these two forms of evidence. Fre-Res preserves sparse high-fidelity spatial anchors and represents dense temporal evolution through compact residual-frequency tokens. Specifically, it applies temporal 1D-DCT to inter-frame residual trajectories in vision-latent space, where we observe strong low-frequency concentration. To align frequency-domain dynamics with native visual embeddings, Fre-Res introduces a Spatial-Guided Absorber that injects temporal residual information into spatially corresponding anchor tokens. Across fine-grained short-video and long-video reasoning benchmarks, Fre-Res achieves a favorable accuracy--efficiency trade-off, matching or approaching full-token performance while substantially reducing visual-token length. Extensive ablations further show that temporal-frequency residuals preserve causal transition cues, while spatial anchors remain essential for fine-grained object and layout reasoning.

</details>


### [7] [GeoSym127K: Scalable Symbolically-verifiable Synthesis for Multimodal Geometric Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16371)
*Jinhao Jing,Zheng Ma,Jinwei Liang,Qiannian Zhao,Shawn Chen,Jing Yang,Por Lip Yee,Prayag Tiwari,Jingjing Bai,Benyou Wang,Lewei Lu,Zhan Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoSym Engine，一个自动化的神经符号框架，用于生成高精度几何推理数据，构建了GeoSym127K数据集和GeoSym-Bench基准，通过SFT和RLVR训练显著提升多模态大模型在几何推理任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在几何推理中常因视觉幻觉和缺乏数学精确的思维链数据而表现不佳。

Method: 提出GeoSym Engine神经符号框架，包含类型条件语法和符号求解器(SymGT Solver)生成精确符号真值，结合渲染管线生成高质量几何图；构建GeoSym127K分层难度数据集（51K图像、127K问题、55K验证CoT QA对）；引入GeoSym-Bench专家精标评估集；采用SFT和基于可验证奖励的强化学习(RLVR/GRPO)进行训练。

Result: Qwen3-VL-8B在MathVerse Vision-Only上+22.21%，在WeMath上达61.52%（+6.19%），超越Doubao-1.8等闭源模型；结构化的SFT checkpoint初始化显著提升RLVR性能天花板。

Conclusion: 自动化符号数据合成结合SFT与RLVR能够有效提升多模态大模型的几何推理能力，确定性精确匹配信号展示了可验证推理合成的鲁棒扩展潜力。

Abstract: Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) often struggle with geometric reasoning due to visual hallucinations and a lack of mathematically precise Chain-of-Thought (CoT) data. To address this, we propose the GeoSym Engine, an automated and scalable neuro-symbolic framework. By leveraging a type-conditional grammar and an analytic SymGT Solver, it derives exact symbolic ground truths and seamlessly integrates with a robust rendering pipeline to produce high-precision geometric diagrams. Using this engine, we construct GeoSym127K, a difficulty-stratified dataset featuring 51K high-resolution images, 127K questions with symbolic ground truths, and 55K answer-verified CoT QA pairs. We also introduce GeoSym-Bench, an expert-curated suite of 511 complex samples for rigorous evaluation. Through extensive supervised fine-tuning (SFT), we demonstrate that GeoSym drives concentrated improvements specifically on diagram-dependent and multi-step geometry tasks. Our Qwen3-VL-8B model gains an absolute +22.21% on the MathVerse Vision-Only subset and reaches 61.52% (+6.19% improvement) on WeMath, mitigating long-horizon logic fragmentation and outperforming advanced closed-source models like Doubao-1.8. Furthermore, applying Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) via GRPO reveals that initializing from structural SFT checkpoints substantially elevates the performance ceiling over zero-shot RL. Driven by deterministic exact-match signals, this showcases the robust scaling potential of our verifiable reasoning synthesis. Datasets and code are available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Tomie0506/GeoSym127K and https://github.com/Tomie56/GeoSym127K.

</details>


### [8] [Cross-Source Supervision for Bone Infection Segmentation in Dual-Modality PET-CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16373)
*Zonglin Yang,Xiaolei Diao,Jishizhan Chen,Xiaozhuang Man,Wei Kong,Gen Wen,Pengfei Cheng,Daqian Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对骨感染PET-CT多模态分割中标注不一致和病灶边界模糊的挑战，提出了一种早期融合双模态端到端分割框架，并开发了解耦双源学习框架以保持专家标注的多样性，采用患者级3D体积评估方法，实验证明多模态融合及多样性保持策略的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 骨感染的早期准确诊断和病灶定位对临床治疗至关重要，但现有PET-CT分割方法面临病灶边界不清晰以及不同专家/自动化系统生成的标注存在不一致性的挑战。

Method: 1) 构建双模态端到端分割框架，通过早期融合方式整合PET代谢信号和CT骨窗解剖信息；2) 提出解耦双源学习框架，并行训练两个分别以高灵敏度和高特异性为临床目标的模型，分别学习独立专家标注；3) 摒弃传统二维评估方法，采用严格的患者级3D体积评估和交叉验证以避免小数据集的层间相关导致的性能膨胀。

Result: 在患者级别以均值±标准差形式客观报告性能变异，交叉评估矩阵定量揭示了模型成功内化不同专家诊断哲学的过程，验证了多模态PET-CT融合的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种鲁棒的、保持多样性的范式，适用于骨感染分割的临床AI部署。

Abstract: Early and accurate diagnosis and lesion localization of bone infections are crucial for clinical treatment. PET-CT integrates anatomical information from CT with metabolic information from PET, making it an important imaging modality for diagnosing bone infections. However, accurate lesion segmentation remains challenging due to indistinct lesion boundaries and inconsistencies in annotations generated by different experts or automated systems. In this work, we investigate multimodal segmentation of bone infections under annotation discrepancy. We develop a bimodal end-to-end segmentation framework that integrates PET metabolic signals and CT bone-window anatomy through an early-fusion multimodal representation.To mitigate performance inflation caused by inter-slice correlation in small datasets, this study discards traditional two-dimensional evaluation methods and implements a rigorous patient-level 3D volumetric evaluation and cross-validation. Furthermore, instead of forcing a singular consensus, we propose a decoupled dual-source learning framework where parallel models are trained on independent expert annotations driven by high-sensitivity and high-specificity clinical intents. Experimental results objectively report performance variations at the patient level (Mean + SD and Mean - SD), demonstrating the effectiveness of multimodal PET-CT fusion. The cross-evaluation matrix quantitatively reveals how models successfully internalize distinct expert diagnostic philosophies, providing a robust, diversity-preserving paradigm for clinical AI deployment in bone infection segmentation.

</details>


### [9] [Mutual Enhancement Between Global Tokens and Patch Tokens: From Theory to Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16384)
*Xiusheng Huang,Xin Jiang,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu,Yequan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于信息熵理论的自适应图像分词框架TaTok，通过引入全局令牌和动态令牌过滤算法，解决了现有方法固定压缩率导致的信息冗余或损失问题，在提升性能的同时实现了8.7倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散图像分词方法对所有内容采用固定压缩率，忽略了图像中信息密度的差异性，导致冗余或信息丢失。

Method: 提出TaTok框架：1）引入全局令牌建模补丁令牌间的互信息；2）提出基于累积条件熵的动态令牌过滤（DTF）算法消除冗余。

Result: 实现了1.3倍gFID提升和8.7倍推理加速，达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 根据信息丰富度分配令牌，实现了更压缩且更准确的图像分词，为未来研究提供了有价值的见解。

Abstract: Accurate and effective discrete image tokenization is crucial for long image sequence processing. However, current methods rigidly compress all content at a fixed rate, ignoring the variable information density of images and leading to either redundancy or information loss. Inspired by information entropy, we propose TaTok, a Theoretically grounded adaptive image Tokenization framework. We rigorously identify two key drawbacks in existing methods: information insufficiency when reconstructing images with patch tokens alone, and information redundancy among patch tokens. To address these, we introduce global tokens that model mutual information across patch tokens, and a Dynamic Token Filtering (DTF) algorithm based on cumulative conditional entropy to eliminate redundancy. Experiments confirm TaTok's state-of-the-art performance, delivering a 1.3x gFID improvement and 8.7x inference speedup. By allocating tokens according to information richness, TaTok enables more compressed yet accurate image tokenization, offering valuable insights for future research.

</details>


### [10] [Hilbert-Geo: Solving Solid Geometric Problems by Neural-Symbolic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16385)
*Ruoran Xu,Haoyu Cheng,Bin Dong,Qiufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Hilbert-Geo，首个面向立体几何的统一形式化语言框架，包含谓词库和定理库；并基于该框架提出Parse2Reason方法（先解析后推理），通过条件描述语言(CDL)形式化表示问题和图形，进行关系推理与代数计算，生成可验证的推理过程。在SolidFGeo2k和MathVerse-Solid上达到SOTA性能（77.3%和84.1%），同时在平面几何上也表现优异（80.2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何推理工作多聚焦于平面几何，由于3D空间图表和复杂推理的限制，在立体几何上通常表现不佳，亟需一个统一的立体几何形式化推理框架来弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出Hilbert-Geo框架（含谓词库与定理库）及Parse2Reason方法：解析阶段使用条件描述语言(CDL)将自然语言文本和视觉图像统一形式化为几何条件表示；推理阶段基于形式化CDL和定理库进行关系推理与代数计算，生成严格正确、可验证且可读的推理过程。

Result: 在SolidFGeo2k上达到77.3%准确率，远超Gemini-2.5-pro（54.2%）；在MathVerse-Solid上达到84.1%，优于GPT-5（62.9%）；在平面几何PlaneFGeo3k上达80.2%，验证了方法的通用性。

Conclusion: Hilbert-Geo作为首个立体几何形式化语言框架，结合Parse2Reason方法显著提升了立体几何推理性能，同时兼容平面几何，展现了良好的通用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Geometric problem solving, as a typical multimodal reasoning problem, has attracted much attention and made great progress recently, however most of works focus on plane geometry while usually fail in solid geometry due to 3D spatial diagrams and complex reasoning. To bridge this gap, we introduce Hilbert-Geo, the first unified formal language framework for solid geometry, including an extensive predicate library and a dedicated theorem bank. Based on this framework, we propose a Parse2Reason method containing two steps of first parsing then reasoning. In the parsing step, we utilize conditional description language (CDL), a formalized language composed of predicates specifically designed to construct geometric conditions, to represent both problem description (natural text) and solid diagrams (visual image). In the reasoning step, we leverage those formal CDL and the theorem bank to perform relational inference and algebraic computation, generating strictly correct, verifiable, and human-readable reasoning processes. Notably, our proposed Hilbert-Geo is also applicable to plane geometry. To advance geometric reasoning, we curate two expert-annotated dataset SolidFGeo2k and PlaneFGeo3k, which are furnished with geometric formal language annotations, solutions and answers. Extensive experiments show that our proposed method achieves the state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance 77.3% in SolidFGeo2k and 84.1% in MathVerse-Solid (one small subset in MathVerse dedicated to solid geometry), substantially outperforming leading MLLMs, such as Gemini-2.5-pro (54.2% on SolidFGeo2k) and GPT-5 (62.9% on MathVerse-Solid). In addition, our method achieves the SOTA accuracy 80.2% in PlaneFGeo3k, demonstrating the generality of the Hilbert-Geo in geometric reasoning. Our code and datasets will be publicly available.

</details>


### [11] [Auditing Multimodal LLM Raters: Central Tendency Bias in Clinical Ordinal Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16386)
*Jiaqing Zhang,Sandeep Elluri,Bhanu Cherukuvada,Yonah Joffe,Jessica Sena,Miguel Contreras,Scott Siegel,Subhash Nerella,Catherine Price,Parisa Rashidi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了多模态大语言模型在临床时钟绘制测试评分中的表现，发现所有LLM家族均存在显著的"中枢趋势效应"——评分被系统地压缩向量表中间值，导致临床关键极端值的评分偏差。尽管零样本GPT-5在容差一致性上表现良好（MAE 0.67，within-1准确率92%），但完全微调的Vision Transformer在绝对校准上更优（MAE 0.52）。该效应无法通过少样本示例或移除临床术语消除，提示在临床高风险场景部署LLM评分器前需要进行校准感知评估和后处理校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型正被探索作为临床自动化评估工具，但其在序数临床量表上的评分行为（尤其是系统性偏差）尚未得到充分理解。本文旨在揭示LLM评分是否存在系统性的结构偏差，及其对临床高风险决策的潜在影响。

Method: 对三个前沿LLM家族（包括GPT-5等）与监督深度学习模型（Vision Transformer）进行基准对比，在两个公开数据集上使用Shulman评分标准对时钟绘制测试图像进行评分。通过绝对误差（MAE）、within-1准确率以及逐得分的细粒度分析来评估校准性能，并设计消融实验（少样本示例跨度、移除临床术语）检验中枢趋势效应的稳健性。

Result: 完全微调的Vision Transformer校准最佳（MAE 0.52，within-1准确率91%）；零样本GPT-5在容差一致性上接近（MAE 0.67，within-1准确率92%）但绝对误差更高。所有三个LLM家族均表现出显著的中枢趋势效应：预测系统性压缩向量表中间值，低端（得分0→1）过度预测，高端（得分5→4）预测不足。该效应在临床关键的极端得分区间尤为突出，且无法通过少样本示例或移除临床术语消除。

Conclusion: LLM在临床序数量表评分中固有的中枢趋势偏差对高风险筛查工作流程构成潜在威胁，研究将"LLM-as-a-judge"偏差文献从NLP评估拓展到临床评估领域，强调在部署基于LLM的评分器前必须进行校准感知评估和后处理校准。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (LLMs) are increasingly explored as automated evaluators in clinical settings, yet their scoring behavior on ordinal clinical scales remains poorly understood. We benchmark three frontier LLM families against supervised deep learning models for scoring Clock Drawing Test (CDT) images on two public datasets using the Shulman rubric. While fully fine-tuned Vision Transformers achieve the best calibration (MAE 0.52, within-1 accuracy 91%), zero-shot LLMs remain competitive on tolerance-based agreement (GPT-5 MAE 0.67, within-1 accuracy 92%) despite higher absolute error. However, per-score analysis reveals that all three LLM families exhibit a pronounced central tendency effect (systematic endpoint compression): predictions are systematically compressed toward the middle of the scale, with over-prediction at the low end (score 0 to 1) and under-prediction at the high end (score 5 to 4). This effect disproportionately affects the clinically critical extremes where accurate scoring most impacts screening decisions for cognitive impairment. Targeted ablations show that neither few-shot exemplars spanning the full score range nor removing clinical terminology from the prompt eliminates the effect. Our findings extend the LLM-as-a-judge bias literature from NLP evaluation to clinical assessment, and highlight the need for calibration-aware evaluation and post-hoc calibration before deploying LLM-based raters in high-stakes screening workflows.

</details>


### [12] [Inducing Spatial Locality in Vision Transformers through the Training Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16390)
*Eduardo Santiago Toledo,Asael Fabian Martínez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了在不使用大规模预训练的情况下，通过训练协议（特别是CutMix）能否促使Vision Transformer（ViT）早期层产生空间局部注意力。实验表明，Modern协议（含CutMix）相比Baseline协议能显著降低平均注意力距离（MAD），从0.316降至0.008（CIFAR-100），消融实验证实CutMix是决定性因素，而AutoAugment和Label Smoothing无独立效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在无大规模预训练条件下，训练协议是否能诱导ViT早期层产生空间局部性注意力，并识别出关键影响因素。

Method: 固定架构和优化过程，比较Baseline协议与Modern协议（包含AutoAugment/ColorJitter、CutMix和Label Smoothing）在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和Tiny-ImageNet上的表现，通过平均注意力距离（MAD）和归一化熵表征每个注意力头，并在CIFAR-100上对每个组件进行消融实验。

Result: Modern协议在所有三个数据集上均产生更局部、更集中的早期层注意力；CIFAR-100上最小MAD从0.316降至0.008。消融实验发现CutMix是决定性组件：含CutMix的条件MAD为0.024，不含CutMix的条件MAD为0.210，而AutoAugment和Label Smoothing无独立影响。

Conclusion: CutMix带来的从局部图像区域进行分类的压力，能够促进Vision Transformer中局部注意力的涌现。

Abstract: We investigate whether the training protocol can induce spatial locality in the early layers of a Vision Transformer (ViT) trained from scratch, without large-scale pretraining. Keeping the architecture and optimization procedure fixed, we compare a Baseline protocol with a Modern protocol (AutoAugment/ColorJitter, CutMix, and Label Smoothing) on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Tiny-ImageNet, characterizing each attention head via Mean Attention Distance (MAD) and normalized entropy. Across all three datasets, the Modern protocol produces more local and more concentrated attention in early layers; on CIFAR-100, the minimum MAD drops from 0.316 (Baseline) to 0.008 (Modern). To identify the source of this effect, we conduct an ablation study on CIFAR-100 by adding or removing each component individually. The results identify CutMix as the determining component within our experiments: all conditions with CutMix exhibit MAD 0.024, while all conditions without CutMix remain at MAD 0.210. AutoAugment and Label Smoothing show no independent effect on locality. Taken together, these findings suggest that the pressure to classify from partial image regions, induced by CutMix, can promote the emergence of local attention in Vision Transformers.

</details>


### [13] [Vision Transformer-Conditioned UNet for Domain-Adaptive Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16393)
*Joel Valdivia Ortega,Tingying Peng,Marion Jasnin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ViTC-UNet，通过可学习token和双向注意力解码器将冻结预训练ViT的全局视觉先验与UNet的局部归纳偏置和高分辨率解码能力结合，在无需端到端微调ViT的情况下，显著提升MRI和CT模态下的语义分割性能，尤其适用于稀疏、细结构和低信噪比目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT在生物医学语义分割中存在性能差距，尤其是在处理稀疏、精细结构和低信噪比目标时表现不佳。作者认为这归因于promptable ViT模型中常用的轻量级像素解码器缺乏局部归纳偏置，难以生成高精度生物医学掩码。

Method: 提出ViTC-UNet，核心思想是通过可学习token和双向注意力解码器，将冻结的预训练ViT表示作为条件输入到UNet中。该方法结合了ViT的全局视觉先验与UNet的局部归纳偏置及高分辨率解码能力，避免了端到端微调ViT，即使在跨领域设置中也无需重新训练ViT。

Result: 在MRI和CT模态的语义分割任务中，ViTC-UNet优于基线方法，验证了结构条件化的UNet解码能够高效地将大规模视觉先验适配到高复杂度的生物医学分割任务。

Conclusion: 结构条件化的UNet解码（ViTC-UNet）是一种高效适配大规模视觉先验到高复杂度生物医学分割的范式，通过融合全局与局部特征，无需微调ViT即可显著提升分割性能。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation is essential for analysing anatomical features in biomedical research, yet a performance gap remains for Vision Transformers (ViTs) in the field, particularly for sparse, fine-structured, and low signal-to-noise targets. We attribute this challenge in part to the lightweight pixel decoders commonly used in promptable ViT models, who may lack the local inductive bias needed for high-precision biomedical masks. We bridge this gap by introducing ViTC-UNet, which conditions a UNet on frozen pre-trained ViT representations through learnable tokens and a two-way attention decoder. This combines ViT global visual priors with the local inductive bias and high-resolution decoding capacity of UNets, while avoiding end-to-end ViT fine-tuning even in cross-domain settings. ViTC-UNet outperforms baseline results in semantic segmentation tasks across MRI and CT modalities, demonstrating that structure-conditioned UNet decoding can efficiently adapt large-scale visual priors to high-complexity biomedical segmentation.

</details>


### [14] [Beyond MMSE: Enhancing PnP Restoration with ProxiMAP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16396)
*Kenta Vert,Giacomo Meanti,Scott Pesme,Michael Arbel,Julien Mairal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出PnP方法中普遍存在的MAP与MMSE去噪器不匹配问题，并发现直接使用扩散模型分数逼近MAP会导致生成卡通化伪影。作者提出ProxiMAP方法，通过维持残差噪声与去噪器训练噪声匹配，使去噪器始终处于分布内，从而获得更锐利的重建结果，并支持即插即用替换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PnP方法中广泛使用MMSE去噪器替代MAP去噪器存在理论不匹配，近期工作尝试用扩散模型分数逼近MAP，但实际中学到的分数有偏，导致收敛到卡通化图像。本文希望解决这一实际失效问题。

Method: 提出ProxiMAP迭代MAP近似方法：设计噪声调度使迭代的残差噪声与去噪器训练噪声保持匹配，保证去噪器始终处于分布内（分数可靠），同时实现隐式早停避免过收敛伪影。还提出混合变体：仅在PnP后期迭代应用ProxiMAP以降低计算成本。

Result: 在去模糊、修复、超分辨率和相位恢复等任务中，ProxiMAP作为标准PnP算法的即插即用替换，一致地获得更锐利的重建结果。混合变体在部分计算成本下达到或超过全替换变体的性能。

Conclusion: 维持残差噪声与去噪器训练噪声匹配是PnP方法成功的关键设计原则，ProxiMAP有效解决了MAP逼近在实际中的失效问题。

Abstract: Plug-and-Play (PnP) methods have become standard tools for solving imaging inverse problems by replacing the intractable maximum a posteriori (MAP) denoiser with the MMSE one. While this mismatch has been widely treated as unavoidable, recent works have sought to close this gap by targeting the MAP with diffusion-model scores. We show this is problematic in practice: learned scores do not match the true ones, so MAP-targeting iterations converge to cartoon-like images rather than realistic ones, and better results are obtained by stopping short of convergence. We turn this observation into a design principle and introduce ProxiMAP, an iterative MAP approximation whose noise schedule keeps the iterate's residual noise matched to the denoiser's training noise. This keeps the denoiser in-distribution where its score is reliable, and yields implicit early stopping that avoids the failure mode above. ProxiMAP is a modular drop-in replacement for MMSE denoisers in standard PnP algorithms and consistently sharpens reconstructions across deblurring, inpainting, super-resolution, and phase retrieval. Building on the same principle, we propose a hybrid variant that applies ProxiMAP only in the late iterations of PnP, where the denoiser is most reliable -- matching or exceeding the full-replacement variant at a fraction of the cost.

</details>


### [15] [Stable and Near-Reversible Diffusion ODE Solvers for Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16399)
*Barbora Barancikova,Daniil Shmelev,Cristopher Salvi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散模型反演在图像编辑中的应用，发现精确可逆的ODE求解器虽能消除反演误差，但在大编辑下存在数值不稳定问题。作者提出使用近可逆Runge-Kutta方法结合向量场平滑策略，在保持背景保留优势的同时，提高了编辑保真度和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型反演在图像编辑中至关重要，但现有可逆ODE求解器在大语义或视觉变化编辑时出现不稳定性和质量骤降。作者希望解决精确可逆性与数值稳定性之间的权衡问题。

Method: 1) 分析精确可逆性与数值稳定性之间的权衡，表现为背景保留与提示对齐的权衡；2) 提出使用近可逆Runge-Kutta方法替代精确可逆扩散方案；3) 结合向量场平滑策略来进一步提升稳定性。

Result: 所提方法在保持可逆求解器背景保留优势的同时，提高了编辑保真度，在大编辑下保持稳定。

Conclusion: 精确可逆性并不足以保证好的图像编辑效果，近可逆方法+向量场平滑能更好地平衡背景保留与编辑质量。

Abstract: The inversion of diffusion models plays a central role in image editing. Algebraically reversible ODE solvers provide an appealing approach to diffusion inversion for text-guided image editing, by eliminating the inversion error inherent in DDIM-based editing pipelines. However, empirical results indicate that reversibility alone is insufficient. As edits require larger semantic or visual changes, reversible diffusion solvers often exhibit instabilities and suffer sharp drops in output quality. In this paper, we show that the trade-off between exact reversibility and numerical stability manifests empirically as a trade-off between background preservation and prompt alignment in image editing. We then investigate the use of near-reversible Runge-Kutta methods as a more stable alternative to exactly reversible diffusion schemes. When combined with a vector-field smoothing strategy, the resulting approach improves edit fidelity, remains stable under large edits, and largely retains the background-preservation benefits of reversible solvers.

</details>


### [16] [CADS: Conformal Adaptive Decision System for Cost-Efficient Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16401)
*Turkoglu Mikael,Bary Tim,Thielens Vincent,Dausort Manon,Macq Benoît*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CADS的序列式多模型算法，利用共形预测在运行时估计数据复杂度，动态地将样本路由到不同规模的模型（从轻量级"侦察兵"到高容量"预言家"），在保持诊断可靠性的同时将计算成本降低多达12倍，有效解决了AI部署中的成本-准确性权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高容量AI模型虽然性能优越，但在实际部署中存在推理成本高、环境影响大以及"一刀切"忽略样本复杂度差异的问题，尤其是在临床场景中，对常规病例浪费计算资源成为可持续AI的重要障碍。

Method: CADS是一个序列式多模型算法，通过共形预测在运行时量化图像不确定性，基于估计的数据复杂度动态选择模型，将样本路由到一个从轻量级"侦察兵"到高容量"预言家"的模型级联中，实现成本与精度的平衡。

Result: 在两个数据集上的验证表明，CADS在计算成本上比高容量模型推理降低了多达12倍，同时保持了更高的效率和准确性。

Conclusion: CADS通过基于实时复杂度精确路由样本，在确保高诊断可靠性的同时，显著降低了AI的经济和环境足迹，为可持续AI部署提供了数学上严谨的框架。

Abstract: While high-capacity AI models have advanced state-of-the-art performance, their practical deployment is often hindered by high inference costs, environmental impact, and a "one-size-fits-all" approach that ignores varying sample complexity. In clinical settings for instance, the waste of computational resources on routine cases is a significant barrier to sustainable AI. In this paper, we introduce the Conformal Adaptive Decision System (CADS), a sequential multi-model algorithm designed to optimize resource allocation by efficiently sampling models based on the estimated data complexity. CADS leverages conformal prediction to quantify image uncertainty at runtime. CADS provides a mathematically grounded framework for balancing the cost-accuracy dilemma that dynamically routes samples through a model cascade, ranging from lightweight "Scout" models to high-capacity "Oracle" architectures. Validated on two datasets, CADS demonstrated superior efficiency and accuracy at a computational cost that can be up to 12 times lower than heavy-model inference. By accurately routing samples based on real-time complexity, CADS ensures high diagnostic reliability while drastically reducing the economic and environmental footprint of AI.

</details>


### [17] [When Vision Speaks for Sound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16403)
*Xiaofei Wen,Wenjie Jacky Mo,Xingyu Fu,Rui Cai,Tinghui Zhu,Wendi Li,Yanan Xie,Muhao Chen,Peng Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了视频多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）中普遍存在的“视听克利弗·汉斯效应”：模型表面理解音频，实则依赖视觉线索推测音频信息，而非真正验证音频流。作者提出Thud干预驱动探测框架（包含Shift、Mute、Swap三种反事实音频编辑）来系统研究该问题，并设计了两阶段对齐策略，仅用1万样本即可在三个干预维度上平均提升28个百分点，同时保持或提升通用视频/音视频QA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频MLLMs虽在音频理解任务上表现良好，但研究发现这种理解往往是视觉驱动的——模型利用视觉线索推断或幻觉声学信息，而非真正验证音频流。这种失败模式在开源和闭源模型中普遍存在，亟需系统性的诊断和解决方案。

Method: (1) 提出Thud框架：通过三种反事实音频编辑（Shift时间同步、Mute声音存在性、Swap音视频一致性）进行干预探测，诊断模型的音频验证缺陷；(2) 设计两阶段对齐策略：先从干预数据构建偏好对训练音频验证能力，再使用事件级通用视频偏好正则化防止过特化。

Result: 最佳10K样本配方在三个干预维度上平均提升28个百分点，同时通用视频和音视频QA基准测试性能略有提升。

Conclusion: 视频MLLMs存在严重的视听克利弗·汉斯效应（视觉驱动音频幻觉），Thud框架可有效诊断该问题，两阶段对齐策略能以极低成本显著提升模型的真实音频验证能力，且不损害通用性能。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress in video-capable MLLMs, we find that their apparent audio understanding in videos is often vision-driven: models rely on visual cues to infer or hallucinate acoustic information, rather than verifying the audio stream. This issue appears across both state-of-the-art open-source omni models and leading closed-source models from providers such as Google and OpenAI. We characterize this failure mode as an audio-visual Clever Hans effect, in which models appear (falsely) audio-grounded, but actually exploit visual-acoustic correlations without verifying whether the audio and visual streams are truly aligned. To systematically study this behavior, we introduce Thud, an intervention-driven probing framework based on three counterfactual audio edits: Shift, which tests temporal synchronization; Mute, which tests sound existence; and Swap, which tests audio-visual consistency. Beyond diagnosis, we further study a two-stage alignment recipe: intervention-derived preference pairs teach audio verification, while event-level general video preferences regularize the model against over-specialization. Our best 10K-sample recipe improves average performance across the three intervention dimensions by 28 percentage points, while slightly improving performance on general video and audio-visual QA benchmarks.

</details>


### [18] [Concepts Worth Having: Refining VLM-Guided Concept Bottleneck Models with Minimal Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16405)
*Nicola Debole,Andrea Passerini,Stefano Teso,Andrea Pugnana,Emanuele Marconato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VH-CBM是一种混合方法，结合视觉-语言模型和少量密集标注，通过高斯过程传播专家监督，在仅标注1%数据时就能比纯VLM引导的CBM预测更准确的概念，并支持主动学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念瓶颈模型（CBM）需要概念级标注，而实际中这类标注很少获得。近期工作使用视觉-语言模型（VLM）自动获取标注，但降低了概念质量和模型可解释性。作者希望在自动化和人工标注之间取得平衡。

Method: VH-CBM采用混合方法，利用VLM嵌入空间中的高斯过程来捕获目标域的全局信息，从而将少量专家密集标注的监督信号传播到任意目标数据点。

Result: 即使仅标注1%的数据，VH-CBM也能比VLM引导的CBM预测更准确的概念，同时具有更好的概念校准性能，并支持主动学习。

Conclusion: VH-CBM通过少量人工标注即可显著提升概念质量和模型可解释性，在标注效率与模型性能之间取得了有效的平衡。

Abstract: Concept-bottleneck models (CBMs) are neural classifiers that compute predictions from high-level concepts extracted from the input. CBMs ensure stakeholders can understand the concepts -- and the predictions they entail -- by learning these from concept-level annotations, which are however seldom available. Recent CBM architectures work around this issue by obtaining annotations from Vision-Language Models (VLMs). While greatly broadening applicability, doing so can yield lower quality concepts and therefore less interpretable models. We strike for a middle ground by introducing Vision-plus-Human-guided CBM (VH-CBM), a hybrid approach that exploits both VLMs and a small amount of dense annotations. VH-CBM employs a Gaussian Process in the VLM's embedding space, which captures useful global information about the target domain, to propagate the expert's supervision to any target data point. Our empirical evaluation shows how VH-CBM predicts more accurate concepts than VLM-guided CBMs even when annotating as little as 1% of the data, while sporting better concept calibration and supporting active learning.

</details>


### [19] [Contrastive-SDXL: Annotation-Preserving Night-Time Augmentation for Pedestrian Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16406)
*Franky George,Muhammad Khalid,Adil Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Contrastive-SDXL框架，利用SDXL-Turbo和LoRA微调，结合基于DINOv2的逐块语义对比损失和对象一致性损失，实现白天到夜晚的图像转换增强，使夜间行人检测的漏检率比仅使用白天数据训练降低6-7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夜间行人检测面临标注数据稀缺和光照差异大的挑战，白天训练的检测器在夜间不可靠。现有潜在扩散模型在图像转换中难以保证检测器相关目标和局部语义结构的保留。

Method: 基于SDXL-Turbo和LoRA微调构建day-to-night增强框架，引入基于DINOv2编码器的逐块语义对比损失（而非生成器编码器特征），利用多层级DINOv2自注意力图强制局部和全局语义一致性，并加入对象一致性损失显式保持行人结构。

Result: 生成图像FID达到22.5；使用合成夜间图像训练的检测器相比仅白天训练的基线漏检率降低6-7%，接近真实夜间数据训练的性能。

Conclusion: 一致性驱动的扩散增强方法能够有效支持安全关键的夜间行人检测任务。

Abstract: Night-time pedestrian detection remains challenging because labelled night-time data are limited and large illumination differences make daytime-only trained detectors unreliable. Latent diffusion models (LDMs) provide a powerful basis for image-to-image translation and cross-domain augmentation, but their effectiveness in safety-critical perception depends on whether detector-relevant objects and local semantic structure are preserved when translating between source and target domains. In this work, we present Contrastive-SDXL, a day-to-night augmentation framework for night-time pedestrian detection built on SDXL-Turbo and fine-tuned using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA). To preserve semantic correspondence between daytime inputs and translated night-time images, we introduce a patch-wise semantic contrastive loss guided by a pretrained DINOv2 encoder rather than generator encoder features. Multi-level DINOv2 self-attention maps enforce both local and global semantic consistency, while an object consistency loss explicitly encourages pedestrian preservation. Contrastive-SDXL produces realistic night-time images, achieving a Frechet Inception Distance (FID) of 22.5. Detectors trained with our synthetic images obtain a 6-7% reduction in miss rate compared with a daytime-only baseline, approaching the performance of detectors trained on real night-time data. These results demonstrate that consistency-driven diffusion augmentation can effectively support safety-critical night-time pedestrian detection.Specific

</details>


### [20] [Visual Search Patterns in 3D Pancreatic Imaging: An Eye Tracking Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16408)
*Anna Anikina,Leila Khaertdinova,Trine Balschmidt,Michael B Andersen,Christoph F Müller,Erik GS Brandt,Henrik S Thomsen,Claudia Mello-Thoms,Bulat Ibragimov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探讨了眼动追踪在三维医学影像（特别是CT扫描）中的应用，提出了一个用于分析放射科医生在浏览CT体积数据时眼动行为的分类体系，并通过两名放射科医生搜索胰腺的案例研究验证了该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 眼动追踪在2D环境下已成熟应用，但在3D医学影像（如CT）中仍面临挑战。放射科医生通常通过浏览数百张二维切片来解读体积图像，缺乏系统的方法来分析他们在三维导航中的眼动行为。本研究旨在填补这一空白，建立眼动数据的分类体系以理解放射科医生的诊断策略。

Method: 提出了一种眼动数据分类体系，将眼动追踪与CT切片导航对齐。通过两名放射科医生搜索腹部CT中胰腺的案例，收集眼动数据并将其与切片导航过程同步，从而在空间和时间维度上可视化医生的注视行为。

Result: 成功实现了眼动数据与CT切片导航的对齐，能够可视化胰腺在体积中的呈现，并在时空维度上分析临床医生的注视行为，为理解放射科医生的诊断搜索策略提供了基础。

Conclusion: 眼动追踪在3D医学影像分析中具有潜力，提出的分类体系有助于系统研究放射科医生在体积图像中的视觉搜索行为，对改进放射科培训和诊断流程具有参考价值。

Abstract: Eye tracking has emerged as a powerful tool for examining visual perception and search strategies in various domains, including medicine. While it is relatively straightforward to apply in 2D settings, its use in 3D medical imaging remains challenging and not yet well explored. This gap is particularly relevant for radiology, where volumetric images such as computed tomography (CT) scans are routinely read by medical experts. Radiologists typically interpret these images by navigating through hundreds of 2D slices, most often viewed in the axial projection. A taxonomy of eye movement data during navigation through a CT volume could be valuable to understand how radiologists approach diagnostic tasks. As an example of the derived taxonomy, we asked two radiologists to search abdominal CTs of the pancreas. We collect eye tracking data and align eye gaze movements with slice navigation to visualize the representation of the pancreas through volume and analyze clinicians' gaze behavior in both space and time.

</details>


### [21] [Multilingual OCR-Aware Fine-Tuning and Prompt-Guided Chain-of-Thought Reasoning for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16409)
*Qinwu Xu,Xin Liu,Yifan Jiang,Haoyu Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种OCR感知的多语言多模态训练框架，结合合成数据生成、LoRA微调和结构化视觉思维链推理，显著提升了MLLM在复杂真实场景下的OCR完整性和多语言翻译准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型在处理真实场景图像时，面对杂乱布局、小字体、模糊、遮挡和复杂排版等条件，OCR和多语言文本理解仍然是主要的失败模式。

Method: (i) 大规模合成OCR到翻译的数据生成；(ii) 基于LoRA的OCR感知监督微调；(iii) 结构化视觉思维链提示用于不确定视觉条件下的推理。

Result: 在多种多语言场景（收据、菜单、海报、标志、手写文本、文档图像）下，显著提升了视觉-文本对齐能力，特别是在提取小字体、模糊、空间分散和部分遮挡文本方面优于基线模型，并与GPT-5类和Gemini系列模型相比减少了幻觉。

Conclusion: 以数据为中心的OCR感知多模态后训练为提高多语言OCR和基于OCR的视觉问答系统提供了一种有效且可扩展的方向。

Abstract: Optical character recognition (OCR) and multilingual text understanding remain major failure modes of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), particularly in real-world images containing cluttered layouts, small fonts, blur, occlusion, and complex typography.
  We present an OCR-aware multilingual multimodal training framework that combines (i) large-scale synthetic OCR-to-translation data generation, (ii) OCR-aware supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with LoRA adaptation, and (iii) structured visual chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting for reasoning under uncertain visual conditions. Using a LLaMA-based multimodal architecture, the proposed framework substantially improves OCR completeness, multilingual translation accuracy, and robustness under degraded visual conditions.
  Experimental results on multilingual receipts, menus, posters, signs, handwritten text, and document images demonstrate significantly improved visual-text grounding compared with the baseline model. In particular, the proposed OCR-aware post-training framework improves extraction of small, blurred, spatially scattered, and partially occluded text while reducing reliance on language priors under uncertain OCR conditions. Qualitative comparisons with frontier multimodal systems, including GPT-5-class and Gemini-family models, further suggest improved OCR grounding and reduced hallucination under noisy and visually ambiguous OCR scenarios.
  Overall, the results indicate that data-centric OCR-aware multimodal post-training provides an effective and scalable direction for improving multilingual OCR and OCR-based visual question answering systems.

</details>


### [22] [Test-Time Hinting for Black-Box Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16410)
*Kaihua Hou,Abhijith Varma Mudunuri,Jiaxing Qiu,Roxana Daneshjou,Thomas Hartvigsen,Ahmed Alaa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Test-Time Hinting方法，通过轻量级提示生成器为VLMs预先生成上下文提示，仅需单次黑盒API调用即可提升视觉问答性能，无需模型权重访问或重复采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM的TTS方法要么需要开放权重访问，要么需要昂贵的重复采样，且主要评估在数学和科学推理任务上，缺乏对通用视觉理解任务的关注。论文观察到VLM错误往往集中在重复的失败模式上，因此希望设计一种更高效、通用的方法。

Method: 训练一个轻量级提示生成器（hint generator），针对给定测试输入预测应预置到提示中的"提示"（hint），提供有针对性的上下文或过程指导，引导VLM避开其特征性失败模式。该方法仅需单次VLM调用和黑盒API访问。

Result: 在自然图像VQA基准上提升了多个闭源VLM的准确率，且这些增益可以泛化到未见过的基准和VLM上，无需重新训练提示生成器。

Conclusion: Test-Time Hinting是一种高效、可泛化的VLM测试时优化方法，通过轻量级提示生成器以单次调用方式提升黑盒VLM的视觉理解性能。

Abstract: Test-time scaling (TTS) methods have proven highly effective for LLMs, yet their application to vision-language models (VLMs) remains relatively underexplored. Existing VLM TTS methods largely require open-weight model access or expensive repeated sampling, and are evaluated primarily on multimodal mathematical and scientific reasoning benchmarks rather than general visual understanding tasks. In this paper, we propose Test-Time Hinting, a method that improves VLM performance via a single VLM call and requiring only black-box API access, which makes it broadly applicable to frontier closed-weight models. Our method is motivated by the observation that VLM errors tend to cluster around recurring failure patterns. We therefore train a lightweight hint generator model to predict, for a given test input, which "hint" should be prepended to the prompt, providing targeted contextual or procedural guidance that steers the VLM away from its characteristic failure modes. We show that Test-Time Hinting improves the accuracy of multiple closed-weight VLMs on natural-image VQA benchmarks and that these gains generalize to unseen benchmarks and VLMs without retraining the hint generator.

</details>


### [23] [Reducing Hallucination in Vision-Language Models via Stage-wise Preference Optimization under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16411)
*Qinwu Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种分阶段偏好优化框架，通过针对性的多模态数据构造（空间关系、OCR不确定性、对抗性错误前提等）来减少VLM中的幻觉问题，实验表明该方法比前沿专有VLM在空间推理和对抗性设置下表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型中的幻觉问题仍然是一个基本挑战，自回归生成在联合概率建模下为了最大化似然，可能产生语言上合理但物理不一致或视觉上无根据的响应。

Method: 分阶段偏好优化框架：1）在已知失败边界附近逐步构建幻觉聚焦的偏好对；2）涵盖模糊空间定向、对象关系、OCR不确定性和对抗性错误前提训练；3）通过最小扰动生成视觉不一致的幻觉负样本；4）使用DPO更好地区分基于推理的回应与看似合理的幻觉。

Result: 在开源基准和真实多模态评估场景中，改进了基础一致性、减少了幻觉、生成了更具信息量的回应。跨模型定性评估显示该框架在模糊空间推理和对抗性错误前提设置下优于多个前沿专有VLM。

Conclusion: 幻觉不仅源于有限的模型能力，也可能源于自回归概率生成在弱视觉基础下倾向于偏好语言上合理的延续。未来工作可探索物理一致性建模、不确定性感知多模态推理以及超越标准自回归解码的架构替代方案。

Abstract: Hallucination remains a fundamental challenge in vision-language models (VLMs), where autoregressive generation may produce linguistically plausible yet physically inconsistent or visually ungrounded responses due to likelihood maximization under joint probabilistic modeling.
  We propose a stage-wise preference optimization framework for hallucination reduction through targeted multimodal data construction. Rather than directly optimizing on generic instruction-following data, our approach progressively constructs hallucination-focused preference pairs near known failure boundaries. The framework emphasizes ambiguous spatial orientation, object relationships, OCR uncertainty, and adversarial false-premise training. Hallucinated negatives are generated through minimally perturbed yet visually inconsistent alternatives, enabling Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to better separate grounded reasoning from plausible hallucination.
  Experiments on open-source benchmarks and real-world multimodal evaluation scenarios demonstrate improved grounding consistency, reduced hallucination, and more informative grounded responses. Cross-model qualitative evaluation further shows that the proposed multimodal LLM DPO framework produces more visually grounded responses than several frontier proprietary VLMs, such as in ambiguous spatial reasoning and adversarial false-premise settings. The results suggest that hallucination may arise not only from limited model capacity, but also from inherent tendencies of autoregressive probabilistic generation to favor linguistically plausible continuations under weak visual grounding. Future work may explore physical consistency modeling, uncertainty-aware multimodal reasoning, and architectural alternatives beyond standard autoregressive decoding.

</details>


### [24] [NERVE: A Neuromorphic Vision and Radar Ensemble for Multi-Sensor Fusion Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16414)
*Omar Mansour,Pietro Martinello,Ethan Milon,YingFu Xu,Manolis Sifalakis,Guangzhi Tang,Amirreza Yousefzadeh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NERVE是一个包含257分钟多传感器同步记录的数据集（DVS、RGB-D、24GHz和77GHz雷达），用于多模态融合的物体检测和距离估计，实验表明DVS+77GHz雷达组合在循环检测器中达到47.5% mAP，距离误差低于1.8m。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集缺乏多传感器（特别是事件相机与多频段雷达）的同步高质量数据，难以支撑多模态融合在动态视觉感知中的研究。

Method: 收集并同步了5种传感器（2个DVS、RGB-D相机、24GHz和77GHz雷达）在办公室环境中的约600GB数据，构建了DVS+Radar子集用于人体检测和距离估计，并采用前馈和循环检测器进行基线实验。

Result: 循环检测器在DVS+77GHz雷达组合下达到47.5% mAP，雷达距离估计的平均绝对误差低于1.8m（以LiDAR为真值）。

Conclusion: 多传感器融合（尤其DVS与77GHz雷达）能显著提升检测性能，NERVE数据集为未来多模态感知研究提供了基准。

Abstract: We present NERVE (Neuromorphic Vision and Radar Ensemble), a multi-sensor dataset comprising 257 minutes of synchronized recordings from five sensors: two Dynamic Vision Sensors (DVS), an RGB-D camera, and two Radar units (24GHz and 77GHz). Captured across 12 measurement days in office environments, NERVE contains around 600GB of uncompressed temporally aligned data with around 914,000 frames and around 9.6 million RGB COCO-formatted annotations covering 16 relevant object categories. To evaluate multi-modal fusion, we construct a DVS+Radar subset for human detection and distance estimation. Baseline experiments using feed-forward and recurrent detectors show that combining DVS with 77GHz Radar consistently improves detection, with recurrent models achieving up to 47.5% mAP and mean absolute Radar distance errors below 1.8m against LiDAR ground truth.

</details>


### [25] [Diffusion Models, Denoiser Architecture and Creativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16415)
*Itamar Levine,Yair Weiss*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过理论和实验证明，扩散模型的"创造力"（生成与训练数据不同的逼真图像）源于去噪器架构与目标分布之间的交互作用，并指出只有当去噪器的归纳偏差与真实目标分布高度对齐时，扩散模型才能成功生成高质量样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型能够生成与训练数据不同的逼真图像（即具有创造力），这一现象令人惊讶，因为理论上贝叶斯最优去噪器只会复制训练样本。作者希望探究创造力背后的机制。

Method: 理论上，针对三种去噪器架构（线性、多项式、瓶颈），给出了生成样本分布关于目标分布和去噪器架构的显式形式。实验上，对流行的UNET去噪器进行微小架构修改，观察其对生成结果的影响。

Result: 理论推导出不同去噪器架构下生成样本分布的显式表达式；实验发现UNET架构的微小改动会导致截然不同的"创造力"表现形式，且往往产生高度不真实的样本。

Conclusion: 扩散模型成功的关键在于去噪器架构的归纳偏差必须与真实目标分布高度对齐；创造力是架构与目标分布交互作用的产物，而非模型本身固有的能力。

Abstract: The creativity of diffusion models refers to their ability to generate highly realistic images that are different from their training data. Creativity is somewhat surprising since it is known that if the denoiser used in the diffusion model is the Bayes optimal denoiser for a given training set, then the model will simply copy the training samples. In this paper we present empirical and theoretical results that suggest that creativity in diffusion models is due to an interaction between the denoiser architecture and the target distribution. Theoretically, we give explicit forms for the distribution of generated samples as a function of the target distribution and the denoiser architecture for three different denoiser architectures (linear, polynomial, bottleneck). Empirically, we show that small changes in the popular UNET denoiser architecture leads to very different forms of creativity, and these small changes often yield samples that are highly nonrealistic. Taken together, our results show that diffusion models will only be successful if the inductive bias of the denoiser architecture is in strong alignment with the true target distribution.

</details>


### [26] [CAVE: A Structured Credit Assignment Approach for Fragmented Visual Evidence Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16416)
*Tengda Guo,Jie Leng,Hanlei Li,Yaoyuan Liang,Qingyue Zhang,Dian Yang,Mingyu Zhang,Yuhua Fu,Shao-Lun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CAVE方法，通过GRPO的结构化过程奖励机制解决视觉语言模型在碎片化视觉推理（整合非局部视觉信息）中的不足，并构建TRACER-Bench评估基准，实验表明该方法在需要碎片化视觉证据整合的任务上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在通用多模态推理上表现良好，但在整合非局部视觉信息以支持语义欠定视觉推理方面仍存在挑战，作者将此挑战定义为"碎片化视觉推理"问题。

Method: 提出CAVE（Credit Assignment for Visual Evidence），一种基于GRPO的结构化过程奖励方法。通过三种互补推理过程信号（信念更新、证据获取、自适应焦点控制）在动作层面评估中间步骤的贡献，指导模型优化每个推理动作。同时构建TRACER-Bench，覆盖四种非局部且语义易混淆的推理维度，并提供关键中间证据来监督推理路径。

Result: CAVE在需要碎片化视觉证据整合的任务上（包括公开基准和新构建的TRACER-Bench）显著提升性能，同时保持通用多模态评估的竞争力。进一步分析表明CAVE有效提高了视觉推理能力，在更长范围和更深跨区域依赖下表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CAVE方法通过结构化过程奖励机制有效解决了碎片化视觉推理挑战，提升了视觉语言模型整合非局部视觉信息的能力，并在长距离跨区域依赖场景下具有更强的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance on general multimodal reasoning, yet remain challenged in integrating nonlocal visual information to support semantically underdetermined visual reasoning. We describe this challenge as Fragmented Visual Reasoning. To this end, we propose Credit Assignment for Visual Evidence (CAVE), a structured process-reward method based on GRPO for interleaved visual reasoning. Specifically, CAVE evaluates the contribution of intermediate steps at the action level via three complementary reasoning process signals: belief update, evidence acquisition, and adaptive focus control, thereby guiding the model to optimize each reasoning action and learn more reliable visual reasoning strategies. Meanwhile, we construct TRACER-Bench, which covers four nonlocal and semantically confusable reasoning dimensions and provides key intermediate evidence to supervise reasoning paths. Experiments demonstrate that CAVE substantially improves performance on tasks requiring fragmented visual evidence integration, covering both public benchmarks and our newly introduced TRACER-Bench, while retaining competitive performance on general multimodal evaluations. Further analyses reveal that CAVE effectively improves the visual reasoning capacity and exhibits stronger robustness under longer-range and deeper cross-region dependencies.

</details>


### [27] [Neural Visual Decoding via Cognitive guided Adaptive Blurring and Information Constrained Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16418)
*Fan Yin,Chuhang Zheng,Peiliang Gong,Donghai Guan,Qi Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CAIA框架，通过认知引导的自适应模糊和信息约束对齐，解决EEG视觉解码中信息粒度不匹配和低信噪比问题，在零样本脑-图像检索任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG视觉解码方法存在两个关键挑战：严重的信息粒度不匹配（视觉信息与神经信号粒度不一致）以及EEG信号的低信噪比。且现有方法通常处理静态视觉特征，忽略了人类视觉的动态选择性和神经振荡的频率特异性。

Method: CAIA框架包含三方面创新：1）基于认知动力学的自适应模糊机制，通过跨模态注意力动态融合中心偏向和显著性引导的视觉线索；2）分布感知边界校准损失，鲁棒地纠正由异常样本导致的对齐偏差；3）认知引导的信息筛选方法，选择任务相关的EEG振荡，利用神经振荡先验和信息瓶颈机制增强信噪比。

Result: 在零样本脑-图像检索任务中，CAIA在主体依赖和主体无关两种设置下均显著提升了Top-1和Top-5平均准确率，大幅优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 优化视觉信息密度以匹配神经粒度是神经解码中更具可解释性和鲁棒性的路径，验证了认知引导方法在神经-视觉解码中的有效性。

Abstract: EEG-based visual decoding aims to establish a mapping between neural signals and visual semantics. However, it remains constrained by the dual challenges of severe information granularity mismatch and the low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) of EEG signals. Existing approaches typically treat static visual features, ignoring the dynamic selectivity of human vision and the frequency specificity of neural oscillations. To bridge this gap, we propose CAIA, a Cognitive-guided Adaptive blurring with Information-Constrained Alignment framework for Neural-Visual decoding. On the visual side, it simulates selective attention to adaptively reduce redundancy. Meanwhile, on the EEG side, it leverages neural oscillation priors and the information bottleneck mechanism to enhance SNR. Specifically, we devise a cognitive-dynamics-based adaptive blurring mechanism that dynamically integrates center-biased and saliency-guided visual cues via cross-modal attention. Furthermore, we introduce a distribution-aware boundary calibration loss to robustly rectify alignment bias caused by outlier samples. Moreover, a cognitively-guided information-screening method is proposed to select task-relevant EEG oscillations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CAIA improves both subject-dependent and subject-independent average Top-1 and Top-5 accuracy in zero-shot brain-to-image retrieval, significantly outperforming prior methods. Our work validates that optimizing visual information density to match neural granularity offers a more interpretable and robust pathway for neural decoding.

</details>


### [28] [Agentic Pipeline for Self-Synchronized Multiview Joint Angle Monitoring in Uncalibrated Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16419)
*Juncheng Yu,Lusi A,Haoxuan Xie,Weiming Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于智能体管线的无标定多视角关节角度监测方法，利用两个无硬件触发的摄像头和 multimodal 大语言模型实现自动视频同步与目标跟踪，在无需标定的家庭环境中实现了与 Vicon 系统相当的性能（MAE=5.97°±2.36°，Pearson r=0.962±0.014）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脊髓损伤患者的长期康复中，运动学监测至关重要。现有基于多视角无标记动作捕捉方法虽有效，但因依赖标定和难以实现多视角同步，难以部署于患者自部署的家庭环境。

Method: 使用两个无硬件触发的摄像头，通过多模态大语言模型实现自动视频同步和智能体驱动的自验证；采用最先进的单目2D姿态估计模型提取候选姿态，并引入基于智能体的选择机制自动识别和跟踪目标对象；将2D姿态通过显式几何建模优化为非标定多视角下的关节角度。

Result: 与 Vicon 系统验证，MAE 为 5.97°±2.36°，Pearson 相关系数为 0.962±0.014。

Conclusion: 该方法有望为患者提供一种实用的、可自部署的日常运动学监测系统，适用于无标定的家庭环境。

Abstract: Kinematic monitoring plays a critical role in long-term rehabilitation for patients with spinal cord injury (SCI), where multi-view markerless motion capture methods have shown significant potential. However, owing to the reliance on calibration and the difficulty of achieving multi-view synchronization, their deployment in patient self-deployed environments remains challenging. In this work, we propose an agentic pipeline for self-synchronized multi-view joint angle monitoring in uncalibrated environments using two cameras without hardware triggers. The Multimodal large language models enable automatic video synchronization and agent-driven self-verification. State-of-the-art monocular 2D pose estimation models are employed to extract candidate poses, where an agent-based selection mechanism is then applied to automatically identify and track the target subject, thereby producing consistent 2D poses in the presence of multiple individuals and occlusions. Such 2D poses are optimized to estimate joint angles from uncalibrated multi-view pose sequences, ensuring interpretability through explicit geometric modeling. Validation against Vicon system demonstrated the strong performance, achieving an MAE of $5.97^\circ \pm 2.36^\circ$ and a Pearson correlation coefficient of $0.962 \pm 0.014$. The proposed method is expected to provide a practical, patient self-deployable system to perform daily kinematic monitoring in uncalibrated home environments.

</details>


### [29] [Video Reconstruction using Diffusion-based Image-to-Video Generation with Trajectory Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16420)
*Stelio Bompai,Ioannis Kontopoulos,Giannis Spiliopoulos,Dimitris Zissis,Konstantinos Tserpes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用预训练图像到视频扩散模型（SG-I2V），结合GPS轨迹引导，无需领域微调即可从单帧参考图像和GPS遥测数据重建无人机拍摄的自主水面船舶结构化机动视频中缺失帧的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自上而下无人机视角下自主水面船舶在结构化海上机动过程中视频帧丢失或缺失的重建问题，尤其是在低纹理、小目标等挑战性海事条件下。

Method: 提出一条流水线：将船上GPS遥测数据通过等距矩形投影映射到图像空间，生成每艘船只的运动线索，作为SG-I2V扩散模型的条件输入，结合单张参考帧生成轨迹引导的视频序列。

Result: SG-I2V在感知质量（BRISQUE 25.52）、运动真实性（时间平滑度1.14，接近真实值1.42）和GPS轨迹遵循度（9.31px）上均优于光流外推和RIFE插值基线方法。

Conclusion: 轨迹引导的扩散合成是海事视频重建在低纹理、小目标条件下的可行方法。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of reconstructing missing or dropped frames in top-down drone video of autonomous surface vehicles performing structured maritime manoeuvres. We propose a pipeline that converts raw GPS telemetry and a single reference frame into a trajectory-guided video sequence using a pre-trained image-to-video diffusion model, requiring no domain-specific fine-tuning. GPS coordinates from onboard telemetry logs are projected into image space via an equirectangular mapping, producing per-vessel motion cues that condition the SG-I2V diffusion model. The generated frames are evaluated against ground-truth video using perceptual, temporal and trajectory-based metrics, and benchmarked against optical flow extrapolation and RIFE interpolation baselines. SG-I2V produces the most naturally appearing frames among all methods (BRISQUE 25.52, closest to ground-truth 23.64), the most realistic motion magnitude (temporal smoothness 1.14 vs. ground truth 1.42), and the strongest GPS trajectory adherence (9.31px vs. 28.70px for ground-truth, the latter reflecting approximate temporal alignment between footage and GPS logs rather than generation error), demonstrating that trajectory-guided diffusion synthesis is a viable approach to maritime video reconstruction under challenging low-texture, small-object conditions.

</details>


### [30] [EAGT: Echocardiography Augmentation for Generalisability and Transferability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16427)
*Soroush Elyasi,Sara Adibzadeh,Nasim Dadashi Serej,Julie Wall,Massoud Zolgharni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过29种数据增强技术及其两两组合的大规模多数据集评估，发现几何变换（特别是仿射、随机水平翻转等）能显著提升超声心动图左心室分割模型的跨数据集泛化能力，而基于强度或伪影的激进增强反而损害泛化性，两两组合优于单一增强，翻转中心组合效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在超声心动图分割中常因跨机构、扫描仪和患者群体而泛化困难，大规模一致标注数据集难以获取。数据增强被广泛用于提升模型鲁棒性，但其在增强超声心动图跨数据集泛化性方面的作用尚不明确。

Method: 在Unity、CAMUS和EchoNet Dynamic三个数据集上，使用U-Net对29种数据增强技术及其两两组合进行大规模多数据集评估，每种增强在多种超参数设置下进行重复实验，通过Dice和IoU指标在域内和跨数据集场景下评估，并用独立t检验量化统计显著性。

Result: 解剖学上合理的几何变换（仿射、平移-缩放-旋转、透视、随机水平翻转）显著提升跨数据集性能；激进的强度或伪影增强通常降低泛化性。两两组合优于单一增强，其中随机水平翻转与仿射的组合在多数迁移场景下持续提升性能。

Conclusion: 为设计增强策略提供了基于实证的指导，以提升超声心动图分割模型的鲁棒性和可迁移性。

Abstract: Deep learning models for echocardiography segmentation often struggle to generalise across institutions, scanners, and patient populations, where collecting large, consistently annotated datasets is infeasible. Data augmentation is widely used to improve the robustness of deep learning models; however, its role in enhancing cross-dataset generalisability in echocardiography remains insufficiently understood. This study presents a large-scale multi-dataset evaluation of 29 data augmentation techniques and their pairwise combinations for 2D left ventricular segmentation using a U-Net trained on Unity, CAMUS, and EchoNet Dynamic datasets. Each augmentation was explored under several hyperparameter settings and assessed through repeated runs using Dice and IoU in both in-domain and cross-dataset scenarios, with statistical significance quantified via independent t-tests. Results show that anatomically plausible geometric transformations, particularly affine, shift-scale-rotate, perspective, and random horizontal flip, substantially improve cross-dataset performance, whereas aggressive intensity- or artefact-based augmentations often degrade generalisability. Pairwise augmentation combinations outperform individual augmentations and show that moderate flip-centric combinations, especially random horizontal flip with affine, yield consistent gains across most transfer scenarios. These findings provide empirically grounded guidance for designing augmentation policies that enhance the robustness and transferability of echocardiography segmentation models.

</details>


### [31] [CT-DegradBench: A Physics-Informed Benchmark for CT Degradation Detection and Severity Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16431)
*Yousra Nabila Taifour,Marouane Tliba,Zuheng Ming,Marie Luong,Nour Aburaed,Aladine Chetouani,Gorkem Durak,Alessandro Bruno,Faouzi Alaya Cheikh,Habib Zaidi,Ulas Bagci,Azeddine Beghdadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CT-DegradBench数据集和SeSpeCT框架，用于CT图像退化检测和严重程度估计。SeSpeCT结合医学视觉语言模型的语义先验和频域特征，无需微调即可联合预测伪影类型和严重程度，在单一和混合退化场景下均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT图像常受多种采集伪影（噪声、模糊、条纹、混叠、金属伪影）影响，但现有CT增强方法仍主要依赖感知和临床有效性有限的图像质量指标评估，且现有数据集仅关注单一恢复任务，缺乏统一的多退化类型基准测试平台。

Method: 提出SeSpeCT框架：1) 利用医学视觉语言模型（如CLIP）的语义先验，通过放射学文本提示在嵌入空间中构建免训练的语义质量轴；2) 结合频域特征捕捉退化特定的频率模式；3) 联合预测伪影类型和严重程度，无需任务特定微调。

Result: SeSpeCT在单一和混合退化场景下均一致优于所评估的基线方法，验证了语义-频谱联合特征的有效性。

Conclusion: CT-DegradBench提供了统一的多退化类型基准，SeSpeCT通过无训练的多模态语义与频域融合实现了有效的CT退化检测与严重程度估计。

Abstract: Computed tomography (CT) images are frequently degraded by acquisition artifacts, including noise, blur, streaking, aliasing, and metal artifacts. Yet CT enhancement is still largely evaluated using image quality metrics with limited perceptual and clinical validity, while existing datasets remain focused on isolated restoration tasks, hindering unified benchmarking across diverse degradation types. We present CT-DegradBench, a dataset and benchmark for CT degradation detection and severity estimation under controlled single- and mixed-artifact settings. CT-DegradBench enables systematic evaluation across multiple degradation families and severity levels within a common experimental framework. We further propose SeSpeCT (Semantic-Spectral CT degradation estimation), a framework that combines semantic priors from medical vision-language models with complementary frequency-domain cues for artifact analysis. SeSpeCT constructs a training-free semantic quality axis in the multimodal embedding space using radiology-informed text prompts, without task-specific fine-tuning, and combines it with spectral features that capture degradation-specific frequency patterns. The resulting representation enables joint prediction of artifact type and severity. Experimental results show that SeSpeCT consistently outperforms the evaluated baselines under both single- and mixed-degradation settings. The framework is available at https://github.com/yousranb/CT-DEGRADBENCH.

</details>


### [32] [Multi-hop Relational Contrastive Learning: Extending Spatial Contrastive Pre-training Beyond Pairwise Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16456)
*Sheikh Tanvir Ahmed,Md. Tanvir Raihan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多跳关系对比学习（MRCL）框架，通过场景图中的k跳路径捕获隐式空间依赖关系，在节点、边和多跳路径上定义多级对比目标，生成空间感知表示，提升内容图谱检索（NDCG@5=0.748）、空间关系识别和图问答等下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比预训练方法仅建模成对关系，未能利用场景图中丰富的组合性和多跳交互信息，限制了场景理解中空间关系的深度表示学习。

Method: 构建场景图结构，通过追踪k跳路径扩展空间对比学习；设计多级对比目标（节点级、边级、多跳路径级），使嵌入在保持物体语义稳定性的同时，对空间布局保持敏感性。

Result: 在GQA子集上，内容图谱检索NDCG@5达到0.748；在空间关系识别和图问答等下游任务上持续获得性能提升。

Conclusion: 多跳关系监督比仅用成对方法提供更丰富的结构指导，生成更鲁棒、更具组合性和几何感知能力的视觉表示。

Abstract: Understanding how objects relate to each other in space is fundamental to scene understanding, yet most contrastive pre-training approaches only model pairwise relationships, leaving richer compositional and multi-hop interactions largely unexplored. We introduce Multi-Hop Relational Contrastive Learning (MRCL), a framework that extends spatial contrastive learning to graph-structured scene representations. By tracing k-hop paths through scene graphs built from detected objects, MRCL captures implicit spatial dependencies that go well beyond what direct object pairs can express. We define a multi-level contrastive objective spanning nodes, edges, and multi-hop paths, encouraging embeddings that remain stable across object semantics while staying responsive to spatial layout. On a GQA subset, MRCL produces spatially-aware representations that improve content-based graph retrieval (NDCG@5 = 0.748) and consistently benefit downstream tasks, including spatial relationship recognition and graph-based question answering. Together, these results suggest that multi-hop relational supervision offers substantially richer structural guidance than pairwise-only methods, leading to visual representations that are more robust, compositional, and geometry-aware.

</details>


### [33] [Conservative AI for Safety-Sensitive Medical Image Restoration: Residual-Bounded CT-CTA Enhancement for Intracranial Aneurysm-Relevant Signal Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16458)
*Weijun Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种残差有界的2.5D图像恢复框架，通过编辑控制图限制修改幅度和空间范围，用于颅内CT/CTA图像的保守性恢复，在保持临床关键区域不变的同时提升图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像恢复模型在安全敏感性场景中必须避免对临床重要区域（如颅内小血管和动脉瘤相关线索）的不可控修改，现有方法缺乏对修改幅度和范围的显式约束。

Method: 将医学图像恢复建模为保守性AI问题，提出残差有界的2.5D恢复框架：模型在原始中心切片上添加学习到的残差，并通过编辑控制图限制修改的幅度和空间范围；使用合成退化的CT/CTA数据进行训练。

Result: 在50个分布外CT-CTA病例上：平均目标增益0.0635，平均PSNR 37.51 dB，医源性编辑率4.0%；1000次蒙特卡洛运行中85.4%保持净正向，无稳定负向案例；外部低剂量CT上方向有益且修改足迹显著小于基线；有意义的编辑集中在脑部和颅骨区域。

Conclusion: 残差有界恢复在边界敏感的血管成像中是可行的，但该结果仅为初步计算证据，未确立临床诊断性能，需要专家审查和前瞻性验证方可临床应用。

Abstract: Image restoration models are increasingly applied to degraded medical scans, but in safety-sensitive settings they must improve image quality without uncontrolled modification of clinically important regions. This is especially relevant for intracranial CT and CT angiography (CTA), where small vessels and aneurysm-relevant cues lie near high-contrast anatomical boundaries. We frame medical image restoration as a conservative AI problem and present a residual-bounded 2.5D restoration framework trained on synthetically degraded CT/CTA inputs. The model adds a learned residual to the original center slice through an edit-control map that limits the magnitude and spatial extent of modification. We evaluate the framework using an aneurysm-relevant image-recovery matrix, paired comparison against a Gaussian baseline, Monte Carlo stability testing, anatomical localization of meaningful edits, and external evaluation on low-dose CT. On 50 out-of-distribution CT-CTA cases, the bounded model achieved a mean target gain of 0.0635, a mean PSNR of 37.51 dB, and an iatrogenic-edit rate of 4.0%. Across 1,000 Monte Carlo runs, it remained net positive in 85.4% of runs with no stably negative cases. On external low-dose CT, the model was directionally beneficial and produced a substantially smaller modification footprint than the baseline. Meaningful edits concentrated in brain and skull regions while unrelated anatomy showed negligible change. These findings provide preliminary computational evidence that residual-bounded restoration is feasible in boundary-sensitive vascular imaging, but they do not establish clinical diagnostic performance and require expert review and prospective validation before clinical use.

</details>


### [34] [REC-RL: Referring expression counting via Gaussian and range-based reward optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16460)
*Hui Liu,Yunlai Teng,Kunlong Bai,Pengfei Qi,Haotian Yan,Liang Li,Junlan Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: REC-RL提出了一种基于强化学习的指代表达计数框架，通过"思考-范围-回答"范式显式优化视觉推理过程，利用分组相对策略优化和两种轻量级奖励（精度奖励和格式奖励），在不依赖额外标注的情况下提升推理质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指代表达计数方法大多依赖基于规则的强化学习，奖励仅关注最终准确率，忽视了中间推理过程的质量，导致模型缺乏上下文感知的视觉推理能力。

Method: 提出REC-RL框架，采用"思考-范围-回答"范式，通过分组相对策略优化算法进行优化；设计两种轻量级奖励：精度奖励（基于范围的区间监督+高斯引导的精度指导）和格式奖励（强制结构化输出）；将中间焦点预测建模为内部决策过程。

Result: 在多个基准上相比强基线方法取得了一致的性能提升，并展现出鲁棒的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过显式优化中间推理过程，无需额外标注即可提升视觉推理质量，且与人类感知更好地对齐。

Abstract: Referring expression counting (REC) is an intention-driven task that requires context-aware visual reasoning. While recent vision-language models incorporate language for visual understanding, most existing REC methods rely on rulebased reinforcement learning with rewards focused primarily on final accuracy, overlooking the quality of intermediate reasoning. We propose REC-RL, a reinforcement learning framework that introduces a think-range-answer paradigm to explicitly optimize the visual reasoning process. RECRL employs Group Relative Policy Optimization and two lightweight rewards: an accuracy reward that combines range-based interval supervision with Gaussian-based precision guidance, and a format reward that enforces structured outputs. By modeling intermediate focus prediction as internal decision-making, REC-RL avoids additional annotations and better aligns with human perception. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines and robust generalization across benchmarks.

</details>


### [35] [SeamCam: Quantifying Seamless Camouflage via Multi-Cue Visual Detectability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16515)
*Amin Karimi Monsefi,Abolfazl Meyarian,Mridul Khurana,Shuheng Wang,Pouyan Navard,Cheng Zhang,Anuj Karpatne,Wei-Lun Chao,Rajiv Ramnath*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动物伪装评估缺乏标准化的定量指标，作者将伪装评估重新定义为视觉定位问题，旨在建立可量化、可复现的伪装程度度量标准。

Method: 1) 提出SeamCam指标：给定图像和目标物种，生成类别条件检测提案，提取分割掩码，找到与真值掩码IoU最高的掩码并集，得分=1-最大可恢复定位信号；2) 利用SeamCam作为DPO偏好信号，微调扩散模型用于伪装生成；3) 构建CamFG-1.5k数据集控制遮挡伪影。

Result: 94名参与者、2,390次对比的人类二选一实验中，SeamCam与人类伪装难度判断的一致率达78.82%，比当前最优方法提升约25%。

Conclusion: SeamCam提供了有效的伪装评估量化指标，可替代人类判断；其作为DPO偏好信号能够以较低成本训练生成伪装图像的扩散模型，而CamFG-1.5k数据集为无偏评估提供了标准化基准。

Abstract: Animals are described as effectively camouflaged when they blend seamlessly with their surrounding, yet no standardized quantitative measure of this seamlessness exists. We address this gap by framing camouflage evaluation as a visual localization problem: a well-camouflaged animal is one that remains difficult to detect even when its category is known. We introduce SeamCam (Seamless Camouflage), a metric that quantifies how detectable an animal is from the available visual evidence. Given an image and a target species, SeamCam generates category-conditioned detection proposals, extracts segmentation masks, and identifies the subset whose collective union yields the highest IoU with the ground-truth mask. The SeamCam score is one minus this maximum recoverable localization signal, where a higher score indicates stronger camouflage (i.e., lower detectability). In a human two-alternative forced-choice study with 94 participants and 2,390 comparisons, SeamCam achieves 78.82% agreement with human camouflage difficulty judgments, outperforming state-of-the-art by about 25%. We then demonstrate SeamCam's utility as a preference signal for Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to fine-tune a diffusion-based inpainting model for camouflage generation. This offers an affordable training approach with an objective explicitly suited for camouflage generation, unlike typical diffusion models. To support rigorous benchmarking, we further introduce CamFG-1.5k, a curated dataset of 1,521 high-resolution images in which animals are fully visible prior to camouflage generation, enabling unbiased evaluation by controlling for occlusion artifacts present in existing datasets. https://7amin.github.io/SeamCam/

</details>


### [36] [DepthPolyp: Pseudo-Depth Guided Lightweight Segmentation for Real-Time Colonoscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16519)
*Zhuoyu Wu,Wenhui Ou,Lexi Zhang,Pei-Sze Tan,Dongjun Wu,Junhe Zhao,Wenqi Fang,Raphaël C. -W. Phan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DepthPolyp，一种基于伪深度引导的多任务学习与高效特征调制的轻量级息肉分割框架。通过层级Ghost分解、交错混洗融合和动态组门控机制，仅需3.57M参数和0.86 GMACs，在移动设备上以超过180 FPS运行，在真实手术视频上优于大20倍的模型，适用于资源受限临床环境。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有息肉分割方法在干净基准图像上表现良好，但在真实临床环境中（如运动模糊、镜面反射、光照不稳定）性能显著下降，缺乏鲁棒且轻量级的解决方案。

Method: 提出DepthPolyp框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 层级Ghost分解用于紧凑特征生成；2) 交错混洗融合实现低成本的跨尺度交互；3) 动态组门控进行自适应分组特征加权。结合伪深度引导的多任务学习进行联合优化。

Result: 在跨数据集泛化实验中，DepthPolyp在干净和噪声目标域上均优于轻量级基线，与大规模模型竞争。在PolypGen真实手术视频评估中，以3.57M参数和0.86 GMACs实现超过180 FPS，分割性能优于大20倍的模型。

Conclusion: DepthPolyp为实时临床部署提供了兼具轻量高效与鲁棒分割能力的解决方案，代码和预训练权重已开源。

Abstract: Accurate polyp segmentation in colonoscopy is essential for early colorectal cancer detection, yet real-world clinical environments pose persistent challenges such as motion blur, specular reflections, and illumination instability. Most existing methods are optimized on clean benchmark images and suffer noticeable performance degradation when deployed in authentic surgical scenarios. We propose DepthPolyp, a lightweight and robust segmentation framework based on pseudo-depth-guided multi-task learning and efficient feature modulation. The architecture combines hierarchical Ghost factorization for compact feature generation, Interleaved Shuffle Fusion for low-cost cross-scale interaction, and Dynamic Group Gating for adaptive group-wise feature weighting. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DepthPolyp achieves strong cross-dataset generalization when trained on degraded data and evaluated on both clean and noisy target domains, consistently outperforming lightweight baselines and remaining competitive with substantially larger models. In real surgical video evaluation on PolypGen, DepthPolyp achieves better segmentation performance than models up to $20\times$ larger while preserving real-time inference speed. With only 3.57M parameters and 0.86 GMACs, the proposed method runs at over 180 FPS on mobile devices, making it well suited for real-time deployment in resource-constrained clinical environments. Code and pretrained weights are available at: https://github.com/ReaganWu/DepthPolyp/

</details>
